# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](http://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/)
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](http://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

<!-- insertion marker -->
## [0.7.0](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/releases/tag/0.7.0) - 2026-11-08

<small>[Compare with 0.6.0](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/compare/0.6.0...0.7.0)</small>

### Features

- Add checked and clock to simulate, check scheduled values, and drop live changes ([bd362ca](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/bd362ca4e32f942a3fed854a7720ce56a814725e) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Nest system classes in a composite by their prepared composites ([8ea1677](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/8ea1677db7ce77a71b7fa207ba3fffbc6816e438) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Make the translated module define a system class on BaseSystem ([527c5b1](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/527c5b1f2b2d3d7103e83db41be56fced1740ceb) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Declare the tags every analysis needs, and check them against the system ([7aa0953](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/7aa09533c9d84d0477d6cfdbd79764b25d6a7abe) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Add MassActionSystem, which epidemic and enzyme kinetics systems subclass ([c724217](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/c7242173e23c72d1f31e53edcda22df0a865dca9) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Read the inventory's time, space, laws, and domains from the tags of each system class ([53479c8](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/53479c8e7542186fb30b442f0e2cc05e2d6a468e) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Tune the operator compiler of the quantum Heisenberg and operator Schrödinger systems ([072c22a](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/072c22af5b5d5969124ca2ebe8c7094f6a76f87c) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Tune invariants, schedules, compilers, and backends of systems for simulate ([6f062ff](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/6f062ffcfd7d0734792a5090973d3a01e94ec8a1) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Read the tuned run settings of a system through get_run_settings, which a system may override ([987498f](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/987498f4c362a7c8bf30660027691b00718ce46f) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Add all_systems, and turn the chemistry factories into system classes ([cacc2cf](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/cacc2cf98e0ef8c39829365ebcbdff19f9df30d7) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Add the time between frames and observers to simulate ([7fd92d7](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/7fd92d766bd771efabb3b2f5170cc1016fa139a8) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Add simulate, which reads tuned settings, chooses solvers from tags, and rejects mismatched solvers and backends ([dcbefe5](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/dcbefe5520c1569abd6eba9c4c0fe9335dc67787) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Add BaseSystem, with derived tags and model parameters passed down to parts, and make System its composite ([f64260a](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/f64260abb7cd0d4f420c21ddc26ab9b19773a2a0) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
- Add capability tags of parts, accepted domains of roles, and supported tags of solvers and backends ([04bea22](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/04bea223e7baac8a70fdba358800354396875f5f) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

## [0.6.0](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/releases/tag/0.6.0) - 2026-11-07

- Add light-emitting robots that cluster with a sensory delay and spread with a prediction ([d748e0b](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/d748e0bee03874996a556e0fe7953d29114ecc52) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

## [0.4.0](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/releases/tag/0.4.0) - 2026-11-05

<small>[Compare with 0.3.0](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/compare/0.3.0...0.4.0)</small>

### Features

- Run one step of a local rule through its law, with linear rules and subshift domains as specs ([a08548a](https://github.com/georgedouzas/scikit-complexity/commit/a08548ac6430be4d37808963afdf50ddfa4fbe17) by georgedouzas). Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
